It runs until ONE OR BOTH are DISPOSED OF: the court's hunt (§9.11), the guild's own hand (each week one keyed band contest of the gang's `bodyStrength` against the guild's — the institution's criminal power read as a might, DRAFT), or exhaustion (supplies). A guild war is the CLOCKED STANDING card `guild_war` (§12) with a weekly line and its CLIMAX the week it resolves: `guild_prevails` (the gang `scattered`) · `gang_prevails` (the guild institution RUINED through `calamityKernel.js :: ruinInstitution` with the closed fate `captured_by_local_powers` — a member of the walker-closed `WORLD_PULSE_FATE_KIND`, no new fate — and the gang `dissolved` into a new guild in its place) · `both_fall` (the guild ruined with the closed fate `disbanded`, and the gang scattered). The condition's archetype word `guild_war` is the card's own word on another surface in the SAME meaning (the war), registered as an aligned pair; `guild_war` is FREE on master.
- **(e) THE PLACE OF A FOUNDING (the owner, 18:46 — "in a different region than the one they terrified (they would have to travel) where their repuation does harm them", read as DOES NOT harm them, a reading the owner may correct; corrected by the owner at 18:47 for the hamlet):** ends (b) and (d) happen in a DIFFERENT REGION from the one the gang terrorized — a town (or its country) where, BY THE GANG'S OWN RECKONING, its name has not travelled: NO DECAYED RING OF ITS OWN DEEDS COVERS the town — the gang's own fame, `fameOf(worldState, { kind: 'body', bodyKind: 'gang', id }, tick)`'s deeds, each decayed and ringed around the place it was done by RN's ONE ring rule `RING_BY_DECAY` (RN §4.3) — the tale's REACH on the road, a fact of the gang's own deeds; one reputation truth, its fade RN's — and whose welcome by law and alignment it BELIEVES at least `tolerated` (`believedStanceOf`, BD12) — its own reckoning errs long, never short: a ring may cover a town that never heard, so the gang goes farther to go straight; this FORCES a base MOVE across country (§5.4's radius re-based; the journey on the road or the ways by the body's own choice, §5.3, exposed to the patrols and the other bodies, §9.1): RN's fade is what makes "going far to go straight" honest. A gang too notorious to be unknown anywhere has only (a) left. End (c) is in place (above).
  - (e)'s ONE READ IS RN's `deedRingAt(worldState, actorRef, settlementId, tick)` (RN §2.3) — "no decayed ring of its own deeds covers the town" = `deedRingAt(worldState, { kind: 'body', bodyKind: 'gang', id }, townId, tick)` returns no ring; BD computes no ring of its own and reads no other actor's belief; (a)'s dispersal reads the same call.

### 6.9 THE OTHER KINDS' ENDS — the symmetries (L-BD 49 (a))
- **A conclave's ends are its creed's row's fates, in the faith system's own words:** ESTABLISHED (its share in the town reaches `STANDING_ESTABLISHED` — the creed is a temple institution there now, BD17; the flock no longer chooses weekly, BD3) · SUPPRESSED back into the patron's fold (its share eroded to nothing; the faith row's own latent `suppressed` state) · SCATTERED (a moving flock beaten, starved or curbed to nothing; its people drift to the towns) · and the flock DRIVEN OUT is not an end but the birth `driven_out` (§3.2). One word each, the faith system's where the faith system owns the fact.
- **A beast's ends:** `starved` (its kills stopped — the brief's "died out") · `ranged_away` (habitat lost to growing towns: no country in its reach holds its habitat for a season — POP's growth shrinking the wild) · `raised` (the DM named it a menace — the brief's "named", BD16).

### 6.10 NAMES AND LEADERS (BD23; L-BD 32 · 32b, the owner's 18:27 and 18:58 words)
- **The name** is DERIVED from place and kind by the voice program's pools ("the Hollow Road gang" · "the Grey Lady's flock at Kell"), NEVER a person's name (the engine names no person) and NEVER the code word "conclave" (six landed name regexes would class it ARCANE — the census hazard); a beast pack is DESCRIBED by its place ("wolves in the Tolle hills"), never named (BD23) (L-BD 32's examples "the conclave of the Grey Lady at Kell" and "the Tolle hill wolves" yield to the census hazard and to the owner's 18:58 word (beasts stay nameless)); shown only once the body is NAMED (`namingOf`, BD23); the DM's own `name` (quoted, never parsed) wins. ROUTE the voice program (the pools' corpus row — the owner's UI + prose release).
- **The leader slot:** a NAMED gang or conclave has ONE OPTIONAL leader, a PARAGON — DM-authored custom content through CH's retainer idiom, the field `paragon.leads` (CH §7: "a sibling field, not a `kind` on the retainer"; the villain who leads the gang; the prophet who leads the flock); CH's field is THE record and BD's `bodyLeaderOf` only READS it (no stored `leaderRef`); `BODY_LEADER` writes through CH's `setLeader`; then the body's weights read the leader's axes (§4.1 (i)), its cohesion rides the leader (§6.4), and a new leader HALVES its lessons (§4.4, HB's seat shock). The paragon's leg MIRRORS the body's (CH §4.2 (3); CH's law: two movers never share a record; the body's place is the leader's destination each week). A polarity turn, the leader's death (the DM's) or the DM's removal (`BODY_LEADER` remove — CH admits no engine step-down, CH §7) drops `leads` — and so the body's leader — at once. Bodies are FACELESS by default; beasts have no leader.

## §7 STANDINGS AND STANCES — how the world meets a body, and how bodies meet each other (BD-2, BD-3; the owner's ~18:1x "gather ties amongst local settlements appropriate for their status", ~18:3x "antagonisti, neutral, or supportive of each other", ~18:5x "a conclave with a good natured diety might have welcome reception … ironically there may be gangs that recieve good purchase in evil chaotic settings")

### 7.1 Court ↔ body — the stance, its default, its hearing
- **Words (closed, per kind):** a court toward a conclave `permitted · curbed · endowed · outlawed`; toward a gang `tolerated · hunted · employed · harboured`; toward a beast always `hunt` (BD10). A row in `courtStances` exists ONLY where the court's hearing or the DM set a word other than the derived default.
- **The CONCLAVE default (`courtStanceOf`, the owner's alignment word):** `d = stanceOf(the town's PATRON god, the flock's god)`; `net = d.cooperation − d.aggression`, shifted DOWN by DRAFT 0.15 where the patron's legitimacy is below `LEGIT_ORGANIC_CONTEST` 0.3 ("a weak patron suppresses rivals harder") and UP by DRAFT 0.15 where the creed already has local followers (a share in the town); `net ≥ 0` → `permitted` (from arrival: NOT a public enemy, NOT pursued by patrols); `−0.3 ≤ net < 0` → `curbed`; `net < −0.3` → `outlawed` (DRAFT bands). A town with no patron god reads `permitted`. `endowed` is NEVER a default: it is the court's act (the hearing, the DM, or a church seat whose own creed it is — then the flock is the patron's, not a conclave). Truth in the default (a court knows its own mind); the BODY's knowledge of it is belief (§5.3).
- **The GANG default (the town's law and alignment, the owner's "good purchase in evil chaotic settings"):** from the town's EFFECTIVE TOLERANCE (`institutionTolerance.js :: effectiveToleranceOf` — "how much cruelty (+) or disorder (+) it will abide", its baseline the patron's conviction): `tol = (cruelty + disorder) / 2`; `tol ≥ 0.3` → `tolerated` (a chaotic or evil court — the owner's "ironically there may be gangs that recieve good purchase in evil chaotic settings"); OTHERWISE → `hunted` (the owner, ~18:3x: "These are public enemies to most") — a lawful-good court and a true-neutral one alike; a PATRON-LESS town (every deity-free world and every town without a patron: `institutionTolerance.js :: patronConviction` — "`if (!patron) return { cruelty: 0, disorder: 0 };`" — reads tolerance 0, which must not read as leave to ride) takes its LAW from its GOVERNMENT: `hunted`, except where its underworld rules (`criminal_shadow_rule`, the faction plane's government, §10.3) — then `tolerated` (pinned for a deity-free world, §16 S42). `employed` is the court's act (its hearing default proposes it where `tol ≥ 0.5` and the court is at war or a rival it believes the gang preys on stands near — "how power works there"); `harboured` is DERIVED where a court that `tolerated`s a gang holds its base while the gang's LIEGE-or-neighbour court `hunted`s it. PL PRICES it ONLY where the LIEGE has ORDERED the hunt — a standing order read by PL's `huntOrderOf` (PL §4.8 (3), OD-PL-57): then a `fealty_defied` row with NO break, REQUESTED through `requestFealtyRow` under the `body:` prefix (PL registers it), the episode `bodyId + ':' + <tick>` (BD composes it), requested only where `bodiesEnabled` ∧ `polityEnabled`; the liege's DEFAULT `hunted` (which every lawful court holds) is only its voice in this court's fork, never a price; a NEIGHBOUR's hunt is BD's pair matter (§10.2 (a)), never a fealty row.
- **The hearing `body_stance` (§12):** minted the week the court's FORK would change the standing word — its inputs the default above, the body's deeds in the court's land (a raid, a sheltering, a fight against a gang), a `supplicate` (a conclave's petition for leave or a shrine), a `bribe`, the liege's ORDER to hunt (PL's `huntOrderOf`, the precedence's call line — an INPUT LINE on this fork, PL §4.8 (2)), a war's need for irregulars (§9.8); and also: the ALMS a far god's flock drew this season, weighted by the patron creed's share and legitimacy (§6.6); a flock's REFUSAL of a demanded sale (ST's `site_sale` `refused` moves the fork ONE STEP toward `curbed`); the court's own LESSON (HB's court row, §4.4 (a): a court that has curbed flocks drifts toward curbing the next); the LIEGE'S FAITH (a member curbing or outlawing a flock of its liege's own creed pays ONE requested `fealty_defied` with its deference patch — "the crown's protection withdrawn one step" is that price's resentment falling through grip into the deference band the liege's relief fork reads, NEVER a protection row, PL §4.8 (4)(c) and §4.9 — §10.2 (b′), the mirror counterforce); the COMMONS' stance (a court hesitates to hunt a body its own people SUPPORT, §7.6); a LEAK that exposes a sponsor's hand in the body (the host court's fork is pressed to `hunted`, §10.4); and an OUTRAGE — a body the court BELIEVES struck an envoy on public mission or a contracted caravan of this court (its telling heard, §9.5 — never the attribution's truth) pushes the court's fork to `hunted` and its bounty fork open (§9.9). A SEIZURE of a flock's site instead of a sale is ST §6.1 step 6c, open ONLY to a court that ALREADY holds the creed `outlawed`, or `curbed` after the flock REFUSED a `site_sale` — NEVER by a stance flip in the same act ("never under `permitted`/`endowed` (the sale only)"); a `curbed` court's seizure turns the creed `outlawed` through BD's writer the week it is taken (the flock hostile toward that court from that week, item 15). DEFAULT: the fork's new word. The DM may leave, tilt (a bias on the fork's `net`, ±0.1 / ±0.3, DRAFT) or decide any word of the subject's line. After the court's act the CONCLAVE CHOOSES (`hide` · `defy` · `move`, §4.2) — the owner's "lie low · defy · move".
- **The covert hire (`employed`):** a court's `employed` stance is a CLANDESTINE HIRE on CH's ONE idiom (`covert: true`; CH §9 "ONE IDIOM, THREE TIES"): the employing court's secret, its leak a registered row of RN's `TELLING_SOURCES` (`leak:body:<bodyId>:<courtId>:<sinceTick>`, `courtId` the employing court, ONE PER TIE — RN's registry spelling) whose CONTENT is CH's leak content `what: 'paragon_leak'` widened with `bodyId` and `bodyKind` (one meaning; CH registers the widening), its exposure fired ONLY through CH's SHARED LEAF `exposeCovertHire(worldState, { subjectId, employerId, workedAgainstId, … })` (CH owns it; it calls `corruptionWeb.js :: applyForeignExposureBlowback` with the ONE source `covert_hire_exposed`; BD's stage calls it and never re-implements; called only where CH's covert arm is lit), ONE LATCH per hire — `exposedAt` on THIS stance row, written by BD's writer in the act that fires it (a row that stayed `confident` would otherwise re-fire weekly); BD NEVER writes CH's `paragonBeliefs` (the belief rows' one writer reads every subject's leak arrivals); ES-6a's leak risk the same. ROUTE CH (the idiom widened to a body subject; no second exposure source) · ROUTE RN (the telling row).
- **Patrols pursue only what their court marks `hunted` or `outlawed`** (and every beast); a `permitted` conclave is NEVER pursued; `curbed` is watched, not hunted (PR's round notices it; no pursuit) (ROUTE PR).

### 7.2 Body ↔ body — the landed labels, a prior by kind and by god, moved by deeds
- **Words:** the landed pair labels `allied · neutral · rival · hostile` (`neighbourSlice.js :: RELATIONSHIP_TYPES`), on `pairs` (one writer). A BEAST holds no stance toward anyone but `hostile` (BD10); every other kind holds `hunt` or `avoid` toward a beast — `hunt` where the band contest's odds favour it, else `avoid`.
- **The prior (written at first meeting, then moved only by deeds):** gang ↔ gang — `rival` where their radii share a road, else `neutral`; conclave ↔ conclave — by `stanceOf(god A, god B)`: `cooperation − aggression ≥ 0.2` → `allied` (sister creeds), `≤ −0.3` → `hostile` (rival gods), between → `neutral`; conclave → gang — by `stanceOf(the god, the villain's axes)` (§4.2): a GOOD god's flock `hostile`, a neutral one `neutral`, an EVIL one `rival` (the landed heuristic: "evil … forms no bloc; its cooperation is transactional and betrayal-priced" — it deals with the gang and never trusts it). The conclave → gang prior is item 13's ALIGNMENT prior on the one landed stance function, never item 9's kind-pair prior ("extortion → hostile, shelter → friendly"); the deeds then move it exactly as item 9 wrote.
- **Deeds move it (one writer):** extortion, a raid on the other's people, a fight → toward `hostile` one step; shelter, a shared enemy fought, a gift → toward `allied` one step; each an incident on the pair (≤ 8, the pair-state idiom); where the body is NAMED (BD23) the act is ALSO a deed on RN's ledger — `body_act`, its DOER the NAMED BODY (`{ kind: 'body', id }`, keyed `g:<bodyId>` or its creed's `creed:<deityRef>`), its leading paragon (CH's `leads`) riding as `ledBy` — one deed, one doer (RN §2.1), never "where a NAMED person is involved". THE REGISTRATION (RN's route (d)): THE ONE PERSON STAGE mints `body_act` from the hearing's CLOSING ROW — the resolved engagement SP-9's docket writes for BD's OWN kinds (`bodies_meet`, a `raid` with a body raider, `traffic_waylaid`, `body_stance`), `{ engagementId, kind, verdict, parties, where, tick, causedBy }` (BD-4's car-0 names the field path once SP-9 lands; `hunt_taken`'s close among them) — and, for the acts that resolve WITHOUT a hearing, from the stage's ACT RECEIPT (§2.7 step 6: `{ receiptId, kind, bodyId, townId, tick, causedBy }`, `recordKey` the receipt's id — RN's path (b)): THE THREE QUARTER ACTS (§7.6) — and BD REGISTERS its closed act kinds as rows of RN's inherent-value table under RN's registration walker (the `paragon_act` idiom: RN owns the derivation and the values, BD the closed list): the body's own — `raided` (a settlement's people) · `robbed_road` (a stretch's traffic) · `fought` (another body) · `gave_shelter` (a flock's shelter of the hunted or of a gang's victims; `sheltered` is the landed `DEFENSIVE_BANDS` word, avoided — `census-r5b.out`) · `merged` · `paid_quarter` and `fed_poor` (toward its own town's people, §7.6) · `robbed_quarter` (the same, the other sign) · `escaped` (it slipped a court's hunt — `hunt_taken`'s close); a LED hunt that closes `beaten` (the riders cut down by the body) is the BODY's deed too — `fought`, its target the hunting court (one act, one deed: the hunt's card is BD's `hunt_taken`, so BD's closing row mints it; a led hunt's clash mints no `patrol_clash` deed) (ruled by delegation, vetoable — the alternative is no deed for a `beaten` close); a COURT's PUBLIC act toward a named body — the `body_stance` words `permitted · endowed · curbed · outlawed · tolerated · hunted` (PUBLIC), never `employed` (covert: it travels only as its leak, §7.1) and never `harboured` (a derived fact, not a declared act); an ARMY's sack of a flock (§9.8 (c)). ONE ACT, ONE DEED: a patrol's clash with a body is PR's `patrol_clash`, a site's verdict ST's `site_verdict`, a hunt's success CH's `hunt_won` (§9.11), never a `body_act` beside them; a NAMELESS body and a beast mint nothing as doers.
- **THE VALUE ROWS BD REGISTERS (RN's walker; RN owns the derivation and may move every value — the `paragon_act` idiom, CH §2.2b's columns):**

  | Act kind | Side | `kind01` | `law01` | `force01` | Target-dependent? | On its face |
  |---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
  | `raided` (a settlement's people) | the body | 0.15 | 0.15 | 0.80 | no (violence on the innocent) | villainous |
  | `robbed_road` (a stretch's traffic) | the body | 0.20 | 0.15 | 0.55 | no (theft on the road) | shameful |
  | `fought` (another body, or a hunt it beat) | the body | 0.50 | 0.40 | 0.70 | yes — read by whom it fought (RN's second reading) | neutral on its face |
  | `gave_shelter` (the hunted, or a gang's victims) | the body (a flock) | 0.80 | 0.45 | 0.10 | yes — sheltering a gang reads as harbouring | admirable |
  | `merged` | the body | 0.50 | 0.50 | 0.30 | no | neutral |
  | `paid_quarter` (bread and protection for its quarter) | the body (a gang) | 0.70 | 0.30 | 0.20 | no (the "good purchase" gang) | admirable |
  | `fed_poor` | the body (a flock) | 0.85 | 0.60 | 0.05 | no | heroic |
  | `robbed_quarter` (it squeezes its own quarter) | the body (a gang) | 0.10 | 0.10 | 0.60 | no | villainous |
  | `escaped` (it slipped a court's hunt) | the body | 0.40 | 0.25 | 0.30 | yes — read by the hunting court's standing | neutral on its face |

  Every value DRAFT, UNMEASURED (the FP-46 roster); an unregistered kind reads neutral until registered.
- **Ties by status (the owner's "ties … appropriate for their status"):** a gang's ties ARE its stances and standings — a fence (its standing in a gate town with a merchant house), a watchman on the take (a `bribe`d court's `tolerated`), a lord who tolerates it, a court that quietly employs it, a SHRINE that shelters it (a conclave's `allied` pair: a gang may LIE UP at an allied flock's shrine — a tie, never a merge; "a conclave never joins a gang"); a conclave's ties are its stances (a court that permits or endows it, sister flocks); a beast has none.

### 7.3 A gang's standing IS its network, composed into the town's criminal power
- **Reads:** the gang record's `standings` (truth: its grip), the town's landed criminal power (`criminalStrength01Of`, the floor).
- **The composition (ONE read, read-time):** `criminalPowerOf(worldState, settlement, settlementId) = 1 − (1 − criminalStrength01Of(settlement)) × Π_gangs (1 − standing_g(settlementId))` — equal to the landed value EXACTLY when no gang stands there (dark: the function returns `criminalStrength01Of(settlement)` before reading anything), saturating, never above 1. Its readers become the landed readers: `supplyKernel.js :: buildSmuggleContext`'s `guildOf` (489 eff; one line), `espionageGauntlet.js`'s `guildEffectiveSecurity(…, criminalStrength01Of(targetItem))` (309; one line), `espionageMath.js :: covertCompetence01(homeSettlement, npc, ctx?)` (163; a defaulted trailing `ctx = { worldState, settlementId }`, its two callers pass it — PLAUSIBLE that `espionageMissions.js`'s call site holds `worldState`, BD-2's car-0 measures). The migration kernel's direct, cycle-guarded stamp read (`originCriminalGrip01`) stays the floor (a route to POP if it should read the composition). Leaf: `src/domain/bodies/criminalPower.js`.
- The brief's "compose INTO it" through the STAMP is REFUTED by measurement: the stamp's writer `thievesGuild.js :: applyGuildToSettlement` is called by the FROZEN kernel only "`if (gStrength) s = applyGuildToSettlement(s, gStrength);`", so a stamp a gang raised could never be cleared when the gang left (the town drops out of the map and the stale stamp stays) — dark-after-lit could not be byte-identical, and the cure needs a kernel line. The composition is therefore at the ONE READ the three consumers share; the corruption plane's guild (captured factions) stays the FLOOR of that read, not a second criminal power. The same stale-stamp path exists on master today when captured factions are freed — a LANDED DEFECT recorded for the corruption plane's owner (ROUTE INT), not cured here.
- **What moves a standing (one writer):** `smuggle` and a raid's `succeeds` in that town's land raise it (DRAFT +0.05, capped 0.6 per gang); a `bribe` raises it at the bribed court; time decays it (DRAFT −0.01 a week, the gang's absence); a LOST fight in the town's land, a `hunted` stance held a season, the gang's end lower it (the end removes it). DEFEAT MOVES STANDINGS (BD8): when the watch or the riders beat a gang, its standing there falls (DRAFT −0.15) and the court's legitimacy and the watch's reputation RISE through the landed verdict reads (`generosityUpdates.js :: applyLegitimacyDeltasToUpdates` — the one legitimacy writer PL's recognition uses; ROUTE INT/PL for the delta row, bounded DRAFT +2 points).
- **Felt:** "The Tolle gang's fences in Kell are doing well; the watch looks the other way." · scrutiny: the gang's network card (towns and grips).

### 7.4 A conclave IS the creed's row (BD3) — its choice leans the faith system's one strength seam
- **The seam:** `religiousContest.js :: advanceReligionStates` computes `strengthByRef[dref] = clamp01(pietyMult * deityLocalStrength({ … }))` per active creed per town, then `advanceShares`. A `cult` creed's weekly choice (§4.2) enters as ONE bounded multiplier on that creed's strength in that town (`× conclaveLean(worldState, cid, dref)`, DRAFT `[0.85, 1.25]`: `preach` 1.15, `rally` 1.05, `defy` 1.1, `hide` 0.9 — the covert congregation's growth is WF-5b's own half-life where lit, `supplicate` 1.0) and the COURT STANCE's bounded multiplier (`permitted` 1.0, `endowed` 1.15, `curbed` 0.85, `outlawed` 0.7, DRAFT) — one line in the host at the strength computation, the factors in BD's leaf `bodies/conclaveLean.js`. The SHARE then moves by the faith system's own law (`SHARE_STEP_MAX` 6, renorm to 100, `standingFor`) — BD writes no share, no standing, no legitimacy. The brief's "the body chooses WHEN and WHERE, the engine no longer rolls them blindly" is read so: WF's spread, rites, saints and the patron contest keep their own rolls (one truth each); what a conclave chooses is its OWN conduct, entering the one strength seam bounded, so the faith system's dynamics stay whole and the choice is legible on scrutiny ("the Grey Lady's faithful preached in Kell this month").
- **A moving conclave's arrival:** `preach` at a town where its creed is absent calls `religionState.js :: attemptEntry(state, deity, newcomerStrength)` at the faith system's own bar (niche, capacity, `PUSH_MARGIN` 1.1, `EVICTION_MARGIN` 1.5), its `newcomerStrength` the body's size band's DRAFT strength × the court-stance multiplier; entered → the body ends `dissolved` and the creed is a `cult` row there (its flock's people are the row's share now); refused → it stays a body (and may `supplicate`).
- **Tier:** aspatial-safe for the in-town lean (the LOCAL lane); the mission and the moving flock need `faithSpreadEnabled` (dramatic and full) and the canon for the road; a deity-free world: dark (no religion subsystem).

### 7.5 The gate, not the curb — the balance of conversion (BD17; the owner's ~19:0x word)
- **Entry gated both ways:** the court's stance (§7.1) multiplies the creed's strength in the town (§7.4) — a `permitted` good flock in a good town grows at the faith system's own pace and its god ENTERS the town's pantheon by share; an `outlawed` flock's strength falls and its standing cannot climb.
- **The two stickinesses (measured, cited from WF's substrate):** entry into the ESTABLISHED set is share-driven and REVERSIBLE (`standingFor`; shares partition) — the natural cap; the PATRON seat is sticky by design (`PATRON_HOLD`, the flip margin, `resolvePatronContest`, `faithUnseatingEnabled` the only roads). BD never touches the patron's hold or the contest.
- **The balance:** a flock scattered or curbed loses its creed's share in that town (by the share law, through the strength lean), its standing falls back, and the patron's legitimacy rises through the landed legitimacy target (`religionLegitimacy.js :: deityLegitimacyTarget` already reads the town's faith context — BD adds no legitimacy term of its own); the NEXT flock's source is the NEIGHBOUR where the creed is established (the mission birth's odds, §3.2: share × piety gap × alignment affinity × the road, at a dwell).
- **The counterforce on curbing:** suppressing a creed that already has LOCAL followers raises their unrest and lowers the court's legitimacy among them — through the LANDED reads: the `religious_conversion_fracture` stressor's own gate (`stressorGates.js :: religiousConversionGate`) gains ONE `{ mult, reason }` row where the court's stance is `curbed` or `outlawed` against a creed with share (DRAFT ×1.3, "The faithful will not be told whom to pray to.") and the court's legitimacy target reads the fracture as it does today — "a town cannot hunt conclaves for free".
- **Power in institutions:** an ESTABLISHED creed is a temple institution in the town's institution count (the urban band's roster; "joining the pantheon" is concretely gaining that institution — PLAUSIBLE until BD-3's car-0 measures that the roster's religious institutions follow the creed's standing; ROUTE WF if the link must be built). The pantheon rank is read, never written by BD.
- **Felt:** "The Grey Lady's faithful came to Ashford from Kell and were welcomed; they keep a shrine by the mill." · "Merewick has outlawed the Grey Lady's flock; her faithful there pray behind shutters, and they are angry."

### 7.6 THE COMMONS' STANCE — a body's cover inside the walls (the owner, 18:43: "Go with your common's stance toward a boy." — the BOARD's verbatim; "a boy" read as "a body") (L-BD 43; measured, `cure/D-BD-r4.md` m9)
- **The words and the one writer:** each town's PEOPLE hold a stance toward a NAMED body present in or near it — `fear · indifference · support` (`COMMONS_STANCES`, §2.6) — and it is NOT a second commons and NOT a second regard: it is RN's regard of the town's people (`s:<townId>`, RN §2.2: "the town's people, the commons") toward the body's key (`g:<bodyId>`; a flock's creed `creed:<deityRef>`), BANDED (`commonsStanceOf`, §2.5). It moves only by the body's DEEDS toward the town's own people through RN's door (the deed source `body_act`, a NAMED body's acts toward a town registered as rows of RN's inherent-value table — RN owns the derivation, BD registers the rows; ROUTE RN): a gang that ROBS its own quarter is feared; one that PAYS and PROTECTS it is supported (the owner's "good purchase" gang); a flock that FEEDS the poor is supported; a pack is ALWAYS `fear` (no key; BD10). INT's commons voice (the grievance ladder `petition · gathering · riot`) reads it where it needs to; BD writes no commons field.
- **THE QUARTER'S DUE — the producer of the three quarter acts** is a weekly ACT RECEIPT, never a hearing (it decides nothing between two parties) and never a new choice word: each week, at §2.7 step 6, a NAMED gang holding a STANDING in its HOME town (`standings[homeId] > 0`) resolves ONE quarter act there by its supply word and its leader's recorded axes (faceless: its town's tolerance): `strained`/`starving` → `robbed_quarter` (it squeezes its own quarter: supplies + DRAFT 0.05); `provisioned` AND a leader whose axes are not cruel (faceless: a town at `tol ≥ 0.3`) → `paid_quarter` (it pays for bread and protection: supplies − DRAFT 0.03, its standing there + DRAFT 0.01); else none; a NAMED flock that `preach`es in a town while `provisioned` resolves `fed_poor` there (supplies − DRAFT 0.03). At most ONE quarter act per body per week, its receipt `quarter:<bodyId>:<tick>`; THE ONE PERSON STAGE mints the `body_act` deed from it (RN's path (b)); a NAMELESS body's is the brigandage or the charity the town's news already tells (no doer). Every number DRAFT, UNMEASURED.
- **What it does — the inside twin of the hidden ways:** a SUPPORTED body HIDES among the people — its COVER in its town's country rises (one bounded term on BD's cover input to THE PATROLS' encounter law, DRAFT +0.2, PR §8.1; ROUTE PR), and a HUNT there costs the court the commons' grievance (THE PATROLS' over-policing term `W_HARASS`, PR §7.5 — cite, never a second); a FEARED body is INFORMED ON — an `informer` sighting row in the town's belief of it (`believedBodyOf`, §2.4) each week it is near, so the court's belief sharpens and the hunt's prediction improves (PR's `predictedPlaceOf` reads the sighting); the stance also feeds RECRUITMENT (`support` is habitat, §2.5).
- **Tier · felt:** the regard arm lights where RN's regard does (`renownEnabled`; RN's person arm where `npcConsequencesEnabled` lights; omniscient presets derive it from the deed ledger, RN §8); "The quarter will not give up the Lantern Street gang; they pay for the bread there." (scrutiny) · "Low Ford sent word of the Tolle gang's camp before the riders asked." (surface).

## §8 THE EMBATTLED REGION — KEEP THE GAUGE, REPLACE THE ENGINE (BD-2; the owner's ~18:1x "this replaces our entire embattled region program where it becomes it and scales the size and numbers of events according to embattled region status and internal security?")

### 8.1 The gauge stays the one gauge
`embattlement.js` — its ramp (`rampThreat` in the frozen kernel), its hysteresis (`ENTER_THRESHOLD` 0.55 · `EXIT_THRESHOLD` 0.30 · `MIN_DWELL_TICKS` 6), its continuous level and its persisted ledger — is UNTOUCHED, a frozen host read by every landed consumer (road losses, routing, migration deaths, supply, the dossier, the map's danger legend (the host's H-rows unchanged), MN's composition). Occupation, siege exhaustion and plague keep their own feeders; no body enters them.

### 8.2 The bodies' row in the ONE presence composition
THE BODIES REGISTER ONE ROW, `bodyPresenceFeeder(T)`, into MN §3.3's `roadCountry(T)` part of `spatial/presenceComposer.js :: presenceTerms` (the shared car — "a later feeder — THE BODIES' road-country row … joins by the same door and nowhere else"), gated by `bodyEffectsEnabled`, read where the canon marker is present. Per country T: the MAX over the bodies whose `where` touches T this week (a body on the stretch `a|b` touches both; a body on a trail touches nothing unless it SORTIED this week; a body at a site attached `at_hand` to T touches T), EXCLUDING a CONCLAVE whose stance with T's court is `permitted` or `endowed` — a welcomed flock presses nothing; a `curbed`/`outlawed` flock presses as a body (EXECUTED, `skeptic/SEAMS-r5-probe/flock-presence.out`: without the exclusion a `permitted` `strong` flock merely based at its shrine read p 0.56 > `ENTER_THRESHOLD` 0.55 and latched its welcoming country alone; ST's S40 lights) — of `PRESENCE_BY_MIGHT[might] × ACT_FACTOR[act]` — DRAFT `thin` 0.35 · `modest` 0.50 · `capable` 0.60 · `strong`/`formidable` 0.70; `ACT_FACTOR` raiding this week 1.0, based in the country 0.8, passing 0.5; a beast in WINTER × 1.2 — so a `capable` raiding body latches a calm, unwatched country on its own (0.60 > 0.55), a `modest` one latches it only with the country's own crime, a `thin` one only sustains a country already embattled (above 0.30). The row sits on MN's SUPPRESSIBLE side: a held keep and the riders take up to the shared cap `σ(T) = min(0.25, siteSuppression(T) + patrolSubtraction(T))` off it — a watched, kept country where a `capable` gang raids reads 0.35 and does not latch WHILE THE TOWN'S OWN RAMP `T` STAYS BELOW ≈0.31 — MN5's union `T + p(1 − T)` crosses `ENTER_THRESHOLD` 0.55 at `T` ≈ 0.3077 (EXECUTED on master's `stepEmbattlement`: `cure/D-MN-r4-probe/union.out`, "ramp T=0.3077 raw=0.5500 → … embattled"), so a town carrying its own war exhaustion or crime latches with the gang's suppressed row on top. The composer caps every row once at `PRESENCE_TERM_CAP` and takes the MAX over feeders, so a body at an ST den does not count twice: where an untied site's holder IS a body, ST's `sitePresenceFeeder` reads zero for that site and the body's row carries it (one term per thing — CARRIED in ST: zero where `bodyEffectsEnabled` is lit, the record's might where it is dark, BD §15). The PER-BODY part of the row is exposed by name, `bodyPresenceTermOf(worldState, bodyId, settlementId) → p01` (`PRESENCE_BY_MIGHT[might] × ACT_FACTOR[act]` for that one body at that town, before the MAX and the suppression), so ST's `denPressOf` reads the one term the composer carries for a body holder and never recomputes it.

### 8.3 The town's crisis follows the bodies — the raider stressor's gate (one row)
`stressorGates.js :: monsterRaiderGate` gains ONE bounded row (the `STRESSOR_SPAWN_GATES` idiom, `[0.1, 3]`, 609 eff): a body raiding or prowling the town's country this week → `{ mult: 1.4, reason: 'A gang works these roads.' }` (gang) / `{ mult: 1.4, reason: 'A pack hunts this country.' }` (beast) / `{ mult: 1.4, reason: 'A hostile flock raids these villages.' }` (a conclave raiding the land of a court that curbs or outlaws it) (DRAFT); so the town's `monster_raider_pressure` — the crisis the world already knows, its migration, its faith disorder, its counterforce — is born where a body actually presses, never in parallel (MN4's idiom: drive the stressor the world knows, never a second crisis). ONE ROW PER BODY per town (the row's `mult` is the largest pressing body's; never a product over bodies), and where that body HOLDS a den ST's own den row for that site YIELDS to it (ST's R2 — `gateResult` multiplies rows, so two rows for one den would double count). The calamity bucket's beast WAVE stays the strike (MN11).

### 8.4 Scaling — bodies cause the level; the level feeds back as habitat
The size and the number of EVENTS in an embattled region are what its bodies DO (§4, §9): more and larger bodies, more acts; a region "scales" because its habitat breeds bodies (§3) and its security kills them (§9.3) — never because a multiplier on the level spawns events. The level FEEDS BACK only as one habitat term (a gang recruits where the roads are already lost, §2.5), bounded by the cap, the dwell and supplies. INTERNAL SECURITY (the watch, THE PATROLS' riders, a held keep under the shared cap, §8.2) is the counterforce on habitat and the killer of bodies.

### 8.5 THE RE-FEED — a declared SAME-SEED SHIFT (THE PROMISE; OD-BD-2, the owner's to sign at its unit)
Lighting `bodyEffectsEnabled` (BD-2) changes same-seed worlds in every preset it lights in: the embattled level gains the bodies' row (countries latch that did not, and stay embattled longer); the criminal power read gains the gangs' standings (smuggling, covert competence and the gauntlet's security move); the raider stressor's births follow the bodies; and — measured (`cure/D-BD-r4.md` m3) — the roads gauntlet's T3 arm (frozen `roadsKernel.js`) fires its `hostage`/`robbed` roll on more hops (it reads `embattlementLevel` against `ROADS_TUNING.EMBATTLED_THRESHOLD`), so more envoys meet bandit roads; and a GUILD WAR's condition (`guild_war`, §6.8 (d)) moves the town's defence readiness and social trust while it lasts (no crime term); and a BOUNTY's funding cost (one step of the court's effective band a season), a HUNT LED's detachment (PR's riders off their round — the unridden round; it pays NO deployment upkeep, §0.1), and the bounty's CALL on CH's board (which paragons and companies answer) all write OTHER programs' ledgers, so they light with `bodyEffectsEnabled` (§13), never with the record alone. The shift is DECLARED here, measured at BD-2's unit (a seed family's level distribution, latch counts and loss totals before and after, at the experience read), and SIGNED BY THE OWNER at that unit — never by delegation. LIGHTING `bodiesEnabled` is itself a same-seed shift (BD22): with `bodyEffectsEnabled` dark, B2 pins what must NOT move — the gauge's row, the criminal-power composition, the gate rows, the bounty's band step, the hunt's detachment, CH's board — while the bodies' own producer rows (a raid's deaths, ST's holders, PR's losses, the legitimacy on a defeat) move other ledgers and are signed at their units. Dark-after-lit: every row reads zero, the composition returns the landed values exactly, the level relaxes on its own hysteresis (the rows read live records only) — and the re-feed's ONE persisted write, the guild war's condition, is no longer re-asserted and EXPIRES within its lease (§6.8 (d), §15.2).

### 8.6 Clock, tier, felt
Weekly, read-last/write-next (the composer reads the body ledger as the stage left it last week); SPATIAL CANON ONLY (the embattlement gate); the OMNISCIENT PRESET's arm the same (no belief). Felt: "The hill road has gone to the Tolle gang; Kell's grain came in short." · scrutiny: the country's card — "embattled: the Tolle gang (a strong band, hungry) raids from the ruin; Kell's riders keep the low road".
