| `bodySponsorshipEnabled` | the clandestine TIE (`sponsorships`, §10.4), its offer and the two kinds `sponsorship_offered` · `sponsor_exposed`, the seasonal delivery and its carriers, the leak's three effects, the cold war's arms (§10.5) | `bodySpillEnabled`; `espionageEnabled` (ES's ONE covert decider — the offer's decision through BD-9's car — and ES-6a's leak) (IN's `infoStatecraftEnabled` gates only §10.5's rumor campaign, never the offer); `covertHire`'s exposure (CH's idiom) | dark: no `sponsorships` key; no tie; lit: a starving gang near a chaotic rival's border accepts an offer and its supplies rise each season; a leak re-aims the patch at the sponsor (S35) | as `bodySpillEnabled` (premium) |

OTHER PROGRAMS' FLAGS GATE FOUR ARMS (none of BD's own): the HABITS at the body grain need HB's `habitConditioningEnabled` (dark: every tilt 1); the COMMONS' stance needs RN's regard (`renownEnabled`; the person arm where `npcConsequencesEnabled` lights); THE RANSOM RACE waits on ES-2b (the owner's slot — dark until it lands; OPENED, by delegation: live design, built after ES's custody wave); the HAMLET end waits on POP's runtime founding (dark until it exists, ⭐ OD-BD-35). Three more: a BROKER's sale of a way needs IN's `informationBrokeragesEnabled` (dark: no market) and, in every preset, IN's own row for the sale (with ES's survey row also absent, FOUR sources live); an operative's SURVEY needs ES's survey product row — both DARK until carried; a way's RUNG moves only where the route lifecycle's `routeLifecycleEnabled` is lit (dark: the candidates at the hidden price); a wartime unit on a way needs WC's chooser arm and PS's declared widening of "armies may not" at their units (dark: armies keep to the roads, PS's quiet crossing for small units stands). Lighting is its own unit for each flag, each with its declared same-seed shift: `bodiesEnabled` alone — its producer rows' writes (a raid's deaths, ST's holders, PR's losses, the legitimacy on a defeat), SIGNED at its unit (B2 pins the effect arms absent); `bodyEffectsEnabled` — THE RE-FEED (OD-BD-2, the owner's); `hiddenWaysEnabled` — routes and catches move (declared at its unit); `bodySpillEnabled` — relationships and factions move (declared at its unit); `bodySponsorshipEnabled` — supplies, relationships and the credibility stock move (declared at its unit). The TIER guidance "canonized ≠ premium" holds: on a map-module world, canonized at its first advance (H22, THE GRACE RULE), the canon arms run from week one where the flags are lit.

## §14 THE WAVES (each: car-0 · leaves · hosts · FIRST-VISIBLE (requirement 16) · TIER (17) · DEGRADED ARM · EDITOR (L10: (a) forks · (b) seals and conditions · (c) the counterparty · (d) the tick and the states · (e) the record and the vocabularies) · Alignment (13) · `causedBy` (20))

BD is a coupling CARRIER across **WAR · POPULATIONS · TRADE · INFO · FAITH** (and the INTERIOR — the commons, the guild, the sword-holder); its prefix `BD` is chartered with its first coupling-registry row (requirement 21(a)/(c)). HBF rows minted, each in its wave's commit: `bodyBirthDraw`, `bodyChoicePick`, `bodiesMeetOutcome`, `bodyRaidOutcome`, `bodySpillDraw`, `guildWarOutcome`, `bodyFissionDraw`, `sponsorDeliveryDraw` (the band contest's draws are the owning kinds').

**BD-0 — THE RECORD, THE MEASURE AND THE DM'S HAND.**
- **Car-0 (a MEASUREMENT before any BD line):** (1) re-measure `calamityKernel.js :: advanceCalamity` with MN's, ST's and PR's leaves and BD's call (461 + 4 × 3 ≤ 800) — ⛔ STOP at build if the host cannot carry a fourth leaf or if MN-0/ST-0/PR-0 chose another host (BD's stage then follows theirs); (2) measure the population: bodies a seed family would hold under the DRAFT caps (cost: DRAFT ≤ 2 ms per tick at fifteen towns, UNMEASURED; a per-snapshot memo on `bodyHabitat`) — and THE PER-TICK COST AT CONTINENT SCALE with the REAL reads (L-BD 49 (c)): the PROXY (`cure/D-BD-r4-probe/tickcost.mjs`, executed: a radius BFS per body, a believed-coverage lookup per stretch in the radius, a seven-way pick, one encounter test per patrol in its country, the world cap one body per two towns) runs 0.16–0.27 ms per tick at 30 towns, 1.4–2.5 ms at 300, 20–38 ms at 3,000 and 78–150 ms at 10,000 (radius two to three hops; linear in bodies × radius area) — ⛔ STOP at build if the real reads at the largest supported world exceed the AGGREGATE per-tick budget; the CAP per region is the first lever, the RADIUS the second; (3) measure ST's untied holders on the family's seeds (how many id-less authored gangs the adoption converts). **Leaves:** `src/domain/bodies/bodyLedger.js` (the one writer; normalizer; ids; vocabularies; `adoptUntiedHolders`), `bodyReads.js` (§2.5), `bodyStage.js` (the stage, filled by BD-1..10), `bodyVerbs.js`. **Host edits:** `calamityKernel.js` 461 (+3: the call and the merge); `realmManifest` (eight rows) and `realmVerbExecution.js` 663 (the apply arms, the `subject` dial with the derived anchor); `display/armyStrength.js` 94 (`STRENGTH_BAND_KEYS` if MN-0 has not landed); `spatialUsage.js` 188 (the `bodies` and `hiddenWays` TRACKED rows). **FIRST-VISIBLE:** the adoption line the advance BD-0 lights on a world with untied bands — "The band at the Tolle ruin is the Tolle hill gang." — and a placed body's dossier line at its approval. **TIER:** canonized (gangs, beasts), aspatial-safe (a placed conclave); premium; the OMNISCIENT PRESET the same. **DEGRADED ARM:** dark. **EDITOR:** (a) forks — none in BD-0 (ids FNV, no draw); (b) seals and conditions — the predicate `bodyPresent(settlementId)` and the eight verbs' predicates (`realmVetoProse` reasons `body_gate_dark` · `body_unknown` · `body_unnamed` — a leader or a name only for a NAMED body, BD23 · `beast_never_named` — a beast takes no name and no leader; a beast may be PLACED nameless, L-BD 36); (c) the counterparty — verbs target real settlements only; every neighbour reader BD adds accepts a phantom partner without throwing; (d) the tick and the states — no institution or person writes; (e) the record and the vocabularies — the ledger is worldState-side lived history outside `rederive`; `BODY_KINDS · BODY_BIRTHS · BODY_ENDS · COURT_STANCES` are save-data contract (a rename withdraws pending decrees through the decree resolver of DESIGN_EDIT_MODE_AND_DECREES §20, item 3: "a pending decree that no longer resolves is WITHDRAWN WITH ITS REASON"). **Alignment:** alignment-empty WITH REASON — a body is not a court and has no constituents (its stances read alignments; they do not move them). **`causedBy`:** the adoption receipt's parent is ST's holder event; a placed body's is the verb's outcome id.

**BD-1 — BIRTHS, SUPPLIES AND THE WEEKLY CHOICE (gangs and beasts).**
- **Car-0:** birth (d) is DARK until WC-11/12's `brigand` event lands (no measurement of the loser's deployment record or the lifted siege's receipt stands in for it: they do not carry the defeat facts, so reading them would mint a second defeat read); measure `MIGRATION_REFUSALS`' outcomes reachable per tick (birth (e)). **Leaves:** `bodyHabitat.js`, `bodyBirths.js`, `bodyChoice.js`, `bodySupply.js`. **Hosts:** none beyond BD-0's stage. **FIRST-VISIBLE:** within a few weeks' advance on a family seed with a frontier town or a hungry quarter — "Out of Kell's quarter, a gang has taken to the hill roads." / "Wolves have come down into the Tolle hills." **TIER:** canonized; premium; the OMNISCIENT PRESET the same (no belief inside the birth). **DEGRADED ARM:** the record without births (BD-0's adoption and the DM's placements only). **EDITOR:** (a) forks `bodies:birth:<word>:<placeKey>:<tick>`, `bodies:<bodyId>:<tick>` — registered, HBF rows `bodyBirthDraw` · `bodyChoicePick`; (b) seals — a DM `BODY_HOLD` pins the choice; the conditions predicate `bodyBirthPossible`; (c) the counterparty — births name their source settlement (real) or site; (d) the tick — births and ends in the stage's fixed order; (e) `BODY_CHOICES` closed per kind. **Alignment:** a conclave's choice reads its god's axes (`stanceOf`, `deityTemper`); a gang's reads the town's tolerance; neither writes alignment. **`causedBy`:** every birth names its source event (the spill's crime-pressure row, the battle receipt, the refusal, the season); every end names its fact.

(scrutiny) — and the leak: "Tolle's silver was found in the Hollow Road gang's den." **TIER:** canonized; `espionageEnabled` (ES's decider); belief presets (the score reads belief); premium. **DEGRADED ARM:** no ties. **EDITOR:** (a) forks `sponsor:<tieId>:<season>` (the delivery's catch; HBF `sponsorDeliveryDraw`) and ES's own keyed hash `es.dispatch.<court>.<tick>:sponsor_body` (the offer's decision — ES's stream, no HBF row of BD's); (b) the two kinds' seals; (c) real courts only; (d) one delivery per season per tie; (e) `CARRIER_KINDS`, `SPONSOR_PURPOSES`. **Alignment:** the sponsor's personality reads its court's axes; none written. **`causedBy`:** a delivery names its tie; a leak its carrier's catch.

**BD-10 — THE LESSONS (the body grain on HB's ledger).**
- **Car-0:** HB-2's ledger landed and lit (`habitConditioningEnabled`); measure the 24-row cap against a body's three classes. **Leaves:** `bodyHabits.js` (the classifier — the body grain's three classes, and the COURT grain's `faith_contested` · `route_menaced` · `crowding_pressed` handed to HB's writer as its ARGUMENT — the action keys, `bodyHabitTilt` with the derived entry seat, §4.4; `BODY_HABITS` the kind's prior). **Host edits:** `habitLedger.js` 134 (+≈4 lines: ONE `ends` change form in the ONE writer `writeHabits` — `changes.ends: [actorId]` drops those actors' rows; its caller list THE BODIES' stage alone, walker-pinned; HB's one prune law gains the actor-ended case there). **FIRST-VISIBLE:** "The Hollow Road gang has learned Kell's riders come on the fourth week." (scrutiny) · "Kell's riders now ride the hill road first." (surface, PR's half). **TIER:** HB's flag; premium. **DEGRADED ARM:** every tilt 1. **EDITOR:** (a) none (HB's curve is deterministic); (b) the DM's `PATROL_ORDER` breaks a round's rut; (c) real bodies; (d) a leader change HALVES them (HB's seat shock, `SEAT_SHOCK_KEEP` 0.5); (e) the three classes HB's. **Alignment:** none. **`causedBy`:** a credit names its act.

## §15 THE LIFECYCLE OF EVERY PERSISTED SHAPE (requirement 22a; a requirement-23 horizon for each)

| Path | 1 `spatialLedgers.bodies` | 2 `spatialLedgers.hiddenWays` | 3 rumor content `bodyId` · `bodyKind` |
|---|---|---|---|
| create | the stage's births and BD-0's adoption; the DM's `PLACE_BODY`; one writer `bodyLedger.js` | a catch, a survey product, a guide, a BROKER's sale, a PURSUIT's gleaned prefix, the DM; `watched` by THE PATROLS' stage; one writer `hiddenWays.js` | the word step of the stage (and the rumor road's carriage) |
| read | §2.5's reads; the composer's row; PR's encounter and coverage; ST's holder reads | `knownTrailsOf`'s readers — PR's coverage and prediction, PS's `hiddenWayFor`, CH's and ES's routing, a wartime unit's risk read, the body's route choice, the map's way layer | `believedBodies` |
| persist | the conditional `spatialLedgers` idiom (drop-when-empty); normalized on load (unknown kinds dropped with a receipt) | the same | the landed rumor record |
| regenerate | lived history: a regeneration keeps the ledger (worldState-side, outside `rederive`); a body whose source settlement no longer exists keeps its record (its `birth.fromId` names history) | kept; a trail whose nodes left the digest is dropped at normalization | kept (the rumor TTL) |
| undo | the proposal ring's snapshot restore (the landed undo); verb inverses are DM acts | the same | the same |
| import | the campaign import carries `spatialLedgers` whole; a body naming a missing settlement is normalized to roaming at its nearest real node | carried; unknown towns dropped | carried |
| migrate | none at landing (a new key); when the map's hidden-ways layer lands, trail keys re-point to the canon's ids (the slot recorded, BD20) | re-keyed at the canon's adoption (the slot) | none |
| dark-after-lit | the ledger STAYS (lived history) and NOTHING READS IT: the composer's row reads zero, `criminalPowerOf` returns the landed value, the gate rows absent, ST's holder refs read as untied holders with the record's `might` (ST's own dark arm) — except a CONCLAVE's site, which reads as a `faith` holder of its CREED (a church's site), never as untied; relighting resumes from the stored records | stays, unread (PR's coverage reads as dark) | stays; the dossier's voice unread |
| death | a body's end writes its `ended` row (ring 24) and drops its record, its pairs and its stance rows; its site passes by ST's ONE writer in ST's words — an ended body's site `cleared` (ST §6.1 step 1), one that left by its own `move` `abandoned`, a `raised` end HANDING it to the menace (never emptied); its HABIT rows drop through BD-10's `ends` form; a SETTLEMENT's death drops that court's `courtStances` and re-homes bodies whose `homeId` it was (to none) | a dead town's knowledge is dropped | untouched |
| veil | a body is in no court's belief until witnessed (BD12); a body at a `keptSecret` site is court-veiled by ST's veil; the DM's view always shows the truth | per-town belief by construction | per-observer by construction |
| horizon (req. 23) | `records` ≤ the world cap; `pairs` ≤ (records choose 2), dropped with a body; `courtStances` ≤ towns × (bodies + creeds); `births` keys dropped one dwell after their tick; `ended` 24 | knowledge FADES on HB's curve and LAPSES below half of one `glancing` credit's distance from neutral under the town's law word unless used, watched or refreshed (L-BD 11f; no fixed term in weeks); the office's ways never fade while the office is held | the rumor TTL |

## §16 THE SCENARIO MATRIX (each row a MEASURABLE assertion, or DARK)

| # | Setup | Assertion |
|---|---|---|
| B1 | all five flags dark; a seed family of canonized and aspatial worlds | every pulse output BYTE-IDENTICAL to master `81f1cd963` (no `bodies` or `hiddenWays` key; `presenceTerms` without BD's row; `criminalPowerOf` never called) |
| B2 | `bodiesEnabled` lit, `bodyEffectsEnabled` dark, 52 weeks | the EFFECT ARMS are absent — `presenceTerms` holds no BD row, `criminalPowerOf` is never called, no gate row, NO `body_bounty` or `hunt_taken` is minted, ST's band and CH's call board are unchanged, no `guild_war` condition is written (the choices' forks are consumption-independent); the bodies' OWN producer rows (a raid's deaths through `applyPopulationOutcomeToSettlement`, ST's holder events, PR's losses, a defeat's legitimacy delta) MAY move other ledgers — a DECLARED same-seed shift measured and signed at BD-0 to BD-4's units; every other ledger byte-identical to B1 |
| B3 | aspatial world, `bodiesEnabled` lit | no gang or beast is ever born; a DM-placed flock chooses weekly; nothing on roads |
| S1 | a frontier town (`monsterThreat: 'frontier'`), forest country (cover ≥ 0.4), spring | within DRAFT 26 weeks a beast record exists with `birth.word === 'wild_born'` and a `birth.placeKey` in the forest country |
| S2 | a city whose criminal power ≥ 0.6, crime pressure high, famine | within its dwell a gang with `birth.word === 'spilled'`, `homeId` the city, might ≤ `capable` |
| S3 | a `capable` gang raiding an unwatched, unkept, calm country; `bodyEffectsEnabled` lit | the country's embattlement `phase` becomes `embattled` the week after (raw ≥ 0.60 > 0.55) |
| S4 | as S3 with a funded sound keep and riders on the roads (σ = 0.25); Kell's own ramp `T` named in the fixture | with `T` < 0.30 the country never latches (`T + 0.35(1 − T)` < 0.55); with `T` ≥ 0.31 (war exhaustion 0.8 → `T` 0.36) it latches the week after (MN5's union; `union.out`) |
| S5 | the gang of S3 killed (`cut_down`) | the level eases and the phase returns to `calm` only after `MIN_DWELL_TICKS` 6 with raw < 0.30; no birth in that country the week of the death; the habitat line still reads above the floor |
| S6 | a gang with standing 0.3 in Kell, Kell's landed criminal power 0.2 | `criminalPowerOf(Kell) = 1 − 0.8 × 0.7 = 0.44`; the smuggling gate's `guildOf(Kell)` reads 0.44; with the gang ended it reads 0.2 exactly (no stamp written: `thievesGuildStrength` unchanged throughout) |
| S7 | a good god's flock arrives at a town whose patron is good (`stanceOf` net ≥ 0) | the court's stance reads `permitted` from arrival; no patrol of that court pursues it; over a family of seeds it takes the ROAD in the large majority of moves and a way only where a toll or a believed army on the road outweighs the prior (the receipt names it) |
| S8 | the same flock at an evil-patron town (net < −0.3), `hiddenWaysEnabled` lit, an unknown way | stance `outlawed`; the flock CHOOSES the way in the large majority of moves (the prior and the zero coverage); on a way no `patrol_engages` is minted while it is unknown; its leg prices at the landed ×2 |
| S9 | an evil-chaotic town (tolerance ≥ 0.3) and a gang | the gang's stance there reads `tolerated`; it rides the overt road in that land |
| S10 | THE MEASURED MATRIX (`cure/D-BD-r5-probe/defence-matrix.mjs` on master's own generated settlements, every might band × every tier, margin 0.2) | a `modest` gang raids some hamlets (6/12 in the probe) and NO walled town, city or metropolis; no `thin`–`strong` body raids a walled town; with a walled town's walls `ruined` (its walls institution's live status), a `strong` or `formidable` body may (1/11 · 11/11) and the card's scrutiny names the collapse; a `formidable` body raids only the rare unwalled town; the same matrix on `monster` for a pack |
| S11 | a beast pack in winter with no kills for three weeks | its row × 1.2 on its stretches; it ends `starved` with an `ended` row and a Herald line |
| S12 | two gangs `rival` in one country, one carrying loot | `bodies_meet` is minted when noticed; its default weights `passed`; `fought` resolves on `SIEGE_VERDICT_BANDS` with one keyed draw |
| S13 | a gang terrorizes a town where a good `warlike` god's flock lives (a `raid` `succeeds` there this season) | the flock's choice set weights `confront`, which SEEKS the gang's believed stretch; met, `bodies_meet` default `fought`; a good `peacelike` god's flock weights shelter (`parleyed` with a shelter incident) and does not confront; a creed's `cult` ROW there confronting reads as a refusal and a warning (one `informer` sighting of the gang in its court's store) and seeks nothing |
| S14 | a neighbour embattled 13+ weeks by a gang based in Kell's land; `bodySpillEnabled` lit | ONE `peace_unkept` incident (`bodyKind: 'gang'`) on the Ashford–Kell pair per body per 13 weeks; resentment up by `m`; under a liege, ONE `peace_unkept` REQUESTED row through `requestFealtyRow` carrying NO patch (the pair's resentment moved once, by the incident) |
| S15 | a feudal town (`noble_patronage`) beats a gang in its land | the NOBLE faction's power moves up by the bounded lean once that season through `applyFactionPatch`; in a `council_rule` town the CIVIC faction's |
| S16 | `PROMOTE_BODY` on a NAMED gang holding a den, then on a nameless one | the card's `name` dial DEFAULTS to the named gang's name and holds the DM's words if the DM changes it; the nameless one's dial is empty until the DM names it; MN's record exists with the body's place (the den's attached country; a way-bound body's node country) and might; the body ends `raised`; the den's holder is the menace, the site not cleared; no other body becomes a menace in 52 weeks without the verb (the engine never spawns a menace) |

| S18 | a long advance (one year) | every week's hearings resolve on the default with their receipts; no body sacks a town; nothing queues |

| S21 | a `thin` gang recruits to `modest` | the week it crosses `NAMING_BAND` it is NAMED (a receipt, no hearing); before that week no town holds a `believedBodyOf` record of it, no Herald line names it and no `body_bounty` is ever minted against it |

| S23 | a good god's flock in the radius of a `funded` town whose patron is good; an `unfunded` town; a far-patron town | `alms_requested` default `given` at a share for the first, nothing for the second (`unfunded`), and for the third a small band AND one input toward `curbed` on that court's next `body_stance` fork; one request per town per season |

| S25 | a provisioned gang holds a `sound` keep; a beast holds a lair; an `endowed` flock holds a `worn` keep | the gang's `siteReadiness01` ≤ 0.45 (`manned` at most) and its repair never above `worn`; the lair's quality only decays; the endowed flock repairs to the patron court's own band |
| S26 | a NAMED `hunted` gang with `bodyThreat01` ≥ 0.5 near a `funded` court | `body_bounty` minted, default `posted`; the call board lists it (`order`); an unfunded court never posts; a `tolerated` gang never carries one; unclaimed it lapses after 52 weeks |

## §18 WHAT A DM READS (the Evidence Law: who, which power, what effect; deeds only, never forecasts; no numerals; no mechanism names)

1. "Out of Kell's quarter, a gang has taken to the hill roads." — surface (one Herald line); scrutiny: its birth card (hungry streets, a thin watch, a strong guild).
2. "Formed from the deserters of Kell's levy, a gang holds the Tolle hills." — surface; scrutiny: the battle it came from.
3. "The hill road has gone to the Tolle gang; Kell's grain came in short." — surface; scrutiny: the country's card (the gang's size and hunger, its base at the ruin, the riders on the low road).
4. "The Tolle gang has come down on Low Ford for its grain." — the raid card (CLIMAX).
5. "The Grey Lady's faithful came to Ashford from Kell and were welcomed; they keep a shrine by the mill." — surface; scrutiny: the two gods' leanings, the court's welcome.
6. "Merewick has outlawed the Grey Lady's flock; her faithful there pray behind shutters, and they are angry." — surface; scrutiny: the followers' unrest, the court's standing among them.
7. "The Grey Lady's faithful in Kell refused the hill gang's demand and sent word to the watch." — scrutiny line on the flock's card.
8. "Kell's riders caught the Tolle gang on the drovers' path; the watch knows the way now." — surface; the trail drawn behind the toggle.
9. "The wolves of the Tolle hills came down to the road in the hard frost." — surface (winter); in spring: "The wolves of the Tolle hills have whelped."
10. "The hill roads are quiet; the quarter still breeds." — scrutiny only (the balance line).
11. "Ashford blames Kell for the brigands in its hills." — surface (a relationship line); scrutiny: the gang's base in Kell's land, the season it has lasted.
12. "With Kell's riders gone to the war, the Tolle gang rides the low road openly." — surface (wartime).
13. "Out of the Tolle hills, the brigands have a name now: the Hollow Road gang." — surface (the naming receipt, once).
14. "Kell has put a price on the Hollow Road gang." — surface (`body_bounty` `posted`).
15. "Kell's warden has taken a troop of riders after the Hollow Road gang; he knows the drover's way." — surface (`hunt_taken` `undertaken`; the troop's size is on the card, never a numeral in the line).
16. "The Grey Lady's faithful asked alms of Ashford; the town gave freely." — scrutiny only (STANDING).
17. "The Hollow Road gang still holds Kell's envoy; the court has refused to pay." — the ransom card's weekly line (scrutiny).
18. "Tolle's silver was found in the Hollow Road gang's den." — surface (the leak).
19. "The Hollow Road gang has crossed the mountains to found a hall of swords where no one knows its name." — scrutiny (an end's line).
20. "The Hollow Road gang is at war with Kell's thieves' guild; the watch stays indoors." — surface (the guild war's first week; its weekly lines scrutiny).
21. "The quarter will not give up the Lantern Street gang; they pay for the bread there." — scrutiny (the commons' stance).
22. "Merewick resents Kell for the priests it sends." — surface (a far flock's `peace_unkept`).
23. "The Hollow Road gang held Kell's envoy through the winter; Kell's silver came to them in the end." — surface (`ransom_granted` at the clock's end).
24. "With the guild and the Hollow Road gang at each other's throats, Kell's watch no longer walks the river quarter." — surface (the guild war's first week: the order that falls, never a forecast).

- OPEN: (5) the owner's 18:46 "where their repuation does harm them" is read as DOES NOT harm them (a reading applied in §6.8 (e)) — the owner's to correct; (6) the hamlet end's runtime founding is new capability (⭐ OD-BD-35) — the owner's; (7) whether a beast placed by the DM (`PLACE_BODY`, OD-BD-25 amended on the owner's L-BD 36 word) should instead stay unplaceable — the first ruling yielded to the owner's approval of item 36; vetoable; (8) ⭐ OD-BD-49 — the ONE token `body` in master's `HIDDEN_PATH_KINDS` widens a landed access law; DECLARED for the owner's sight (one network ruled); (9) OD-BD-52 — the guild war carried by ONE active condition (the one channel the security read consumes, measured) rather than a war-keyed `criminal_corridor` stressor record (MN4's idiom), which on master would reach the security read only through the same archetype map that collapses it to `regional_criminal_pressure` (no `defense_readiness`) — the stressor is the alternative, and then the archetype map needs its own row (vetoable); (10) the bypass candidates (§5.2 (ii)) are the host's fallback geometry — the map may draw its own and supersede them; (11) the conclave's `raid` weight for a GOOD god's starving, hunted flock is DRAFT ×0.25 ("rarely and in extremity, never never") — the owner's to tune; (12) ⭐ OD-BD-56 — the route MODE `way` and its two fields are a persisted-shape change on the landed route ledger, the owner's; (13) OD-BD-57 — the condition carries the owner's "drastically increases crime" by the war's own bodies and two criminal powers, never by a raised crime pressure (a reading, visible); (14) OD-BD-60 — a led hunt's `beaten` close as the body's `fought` deed (RN's open question, ruled here by delegation).

### 19.2 Slots named here, built elsewhere (L-BD 49 (b) · (d))
- **THE DOSSIER'S DESK for the quarter and the hills** (the voice program's corpus; the owner's UI + prose release): BD names the FACTS the desk may read, never the prose — a NAMED body's name, kind, might word, supply word (`provisioned · strained · starving`), base, the court's stance toward it, the commons' stance, its last believed act (`believedBodyOf`), its leader if known, its end goal on scrutiny, a standing bounty, a hunt out against it; for a nameless body only the brigandage or the piety it feeds.

BD mints NO stressor kind, NO condition and NO crisis law; every row below rides a landed record or is a named SLOT.
